}

country_event = {
	id = ita_dnc.401
	title = ita_dnc.401.t
	desc = ita_dnc.401.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.401.a
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = ITA_arm_promotion_by_merit
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
		add_ideas = ITA_arm_promotion_by_examination
		army_experience = 25
		set_country_flag = ITA_arm_promotion_by_merit
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character = ITA_alberto_pollio
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_arm_pollio_at_the_boards_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				every_unit_leader = {
					limit = {
						is_character = ITA_alberto_pollio
					}
					add_skill_level = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.401.b
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = ITA_arm_seniority_is_kept
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
		add_ideas = ITA_arm_the_seniority_list
		set_country_flag = ITA_arm_seniority_is_kept
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character = ITA_luigi_cadorna
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_arm_cadorna_waits_his_turn_tt
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ita_dnc.405
	title = ita_dnc.405.t
	desc = ita_dnc.405.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_military_parade
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.405.a
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
		army_experience = 25
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_arm_robilant_at_the_staff_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character = ITA_mario_di_robilant
			}
			add_advisor_role = {
				character = ITA_mario_di_robilant
				advisor = {
					slot = high_command
					idea_token = ITA_arm_robilant_at_the_staff
					ledger = army
					traits = { army_commando_2 }
					cost = 100
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_template = "Brigata Alpina" }
			}
			division_template = {
				name = "Brigata Alpina"
				regiments = {
					mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 0 }
					mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 1 }
					mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				}
				support = {
					recon = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				}
				priority = 1
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ITA_arm_the_alpini_have_an_establishment
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.405.b
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
		add_manpower = 20000
		set_country_flag = ITA_arm_the_alpini_stay_line_battalions
	}
}

	picture = GFX_report_event_merchant_ship_01
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.1997.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 3
			modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ITA_g_gentiloni
					has_idea = ITA_a_the_widened_franchise
					has_country_flag = ITA_g_powers_asked
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.3 has_country_flag = ITA_g_the_north_is_lost }
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = 40 producer = ITA }
		set_cosmetic_tag = ITA_KINGDOM
		set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic elections_allowed = yes }
		promote_character = ITA_giovanni_giolitti
		set_party_name = { ideology = democratic name = ITA_g_unione_liberale long_name = ITA_g_unione_liberale_long }
		add_ideas = { ITA_end_the_state_that_chose }
		set_country_flag = ITA_dnc_end_giolitti
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.1997.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 50 compare = less_than }
					has_country_flag = ITA_g_the_north_is_lost
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ITA_g_gentiloni }
				NOT = { has_idea = ITA_a_the_widened_franchise }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ITA_g_powers_asked }
			}
		}
		set_cosmetic_tag = ITA_KINGDOM
		set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic elections_allowed = yes }
		promote_character = ITA_sidney_sonnino
		set_party_name = { ideology = democratic name = ITA_g_destra_costituzionale long_name = ITA_g_destra_costituzionale_long }
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = ITA_a_the_giolittian_system }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = ITA_a_the_giolittian_system add_idea = ITA_g_the_system_outlived_its_arithmetic }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_idea = ITA_a_the_majority_that_governs }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = ITA_a_the_majority_that_governs add_idea = ITA_g_the_system_outlived_its_arithmetic }
		}
		else = {
			add_ideas = { ITA_g_the_system_outlived_its_arithmetic }
		}
		add_ideas = { ITA_end_the_accidental_ministry }
		set_country_flag = ITA_dnc_end_sonnino_interlude
	}
}

				ITA = {
					has_country_flag = ITA_w_alliance_renewed

				ITA = { check_variable = { var = ITA_crown value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			}

		}

			add_state_claim = 458
			add_state_claim = 665
			set_country_flag = ITA_s_berlin_backs_tunis

			add_named_threat = { threat = 2 name = ITA_s_threat_rome_looks_at_tunis }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRA }

		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = AUH }
			AUH = { country_event = { id = ita_dnc.1751 days = 6 } }
		}
	}

		name = ita_dnc.1748.b

			base = 2

				factor = 2
				ITA = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ITA_w_the_timetable_is_agreed } }
			}
		}

				factor = 3

					OR = {
						has_country_flag = ITA_w_note_leaked
						has_country_flag = ITA_w_alliance_denounced

			}
		}
		ITA = {

			set_country_flag = ITA_s_berlin_declined_tunis
		}
	}
}

	fire_only_once = yes

		name = ita_dnc.1880.a

					check_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 55 compare = less_than }
					has_country_flag = ITA_r_pope_guaranteed
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.3 NOT = { has_country_flag = ITA_republic } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_country_flag = ITA_r_pope_stays }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ITA_republic } }
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = democratic
				elections_allowed = yes
				election_frequency = 48
			}
			set_cosmetic_tag = ITA_REP
			promote_character = { character = ITA_napoleone_colajanni ideology = democratic_republicanism }

				ideology = democratic
				name = ITA_r_partito_repubblicano
				long_name = ITA_r_partito_repubblicano_long
			}
			set_country_flag = ITA_republic
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = ITA_r_the_constituent_sits }
			remove_ideas = { ITA_r_the_constituent_sits }
		}
		add_ideas = { ITA_r_the_republic_one_and_indivisible }
		set_country_flag = ITA_dnc_end_republic
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = ENG }
			ENG = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = large_increase } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = USA }
			USA = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = large_increase } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = AUH }
			AUH = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = medium_decrease } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = GER }
			GER = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = medium_decrease } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = RUS }
			RUS = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = medium_decrease } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.1880.b
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ITA_r_syndicalists_radical
				check_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_country_flag = ITA_r_syndicalists_radical
				check_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 3 NOT = { has_country_flag = ITA_republic } }
		}
		promote_character = { character = ITA_arturo_labriola ideology = syndicalism }
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = communism
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = communism
			name = ITA_r_camere_del_lavoro
			long_name = ITA_r_camere_del_lavoro_long
		}

			limit = { NOT = { has_idea = ITA_r_the_eight_hours } }
			add_ideas = { ITA_r_the_eight_hours }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = ITA_r_the_constituent_sits }
			remove_ideas = { ITA_r_the_constituent_sits }
		}
		set_country_flag = ITA_dnc_end_syndicalist
		add_named_threat = { threat = 3 name = ITA_r_threat_the_republic_of_labour }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = ENG }
			ENG = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = small_increase } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = USA }
			USA = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = small_increase } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRA }
			FRA = { country_event = { id = ita_dnc.1883 days = 5 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = AUH }
			AUH = { country_event = { id = ita_dnc.1882 days = 6 } }
		}
		if = {

		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = RUS }

		}

			modifier = { factor = 0.3 has_country_flag = ITA_k_bloc_in_the_street }
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_country_flag = ITA_k_murri_dropped }
		}
		2 = { remove_core_of = PAP }
		157 = { remove_core_of = PAP }
		161 = { remove_core_of = PAP }
		990 = { remove_core_of = PAP }

			transfer_state = 828
			set_capital = { state = 828 }
			set_cosmetic_tag = PAP_VATICAN
		}

			limit = { has_country_flag = ITA_ch_pius_x }
			PAP = {
				retire_character = PAP_leo_xiii
				recruit_character = PAP_pius_x
				promote_character = { character = PAP_pius_x ideology = christianity }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ITA_ch_rampolla }
			PAP = {
				create_country_leader = {
					name = "Mariano Rampolla del Tindaro"
					picture = "gfx/leaders/PAP/WW1/HolySee_PopeBenedictXV.dds"
					ideology = christianity
					traits = { }
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = ITA_the_non_expedit }
			remove_ideas = { ITA_the_non_expedit }
		}
		add_ideas = { ITA_k_the_conciliation }

		set_cosmetic_tag = ITA_CONC

			limit = { country_exists = FRA }

		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = AUH }
			AUH = { country_event = { id = ita_dnc.2074 days = 5 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = GER }
			GER = { country_event = { id = ita_dnc.2074 days = 5 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.2072.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = ITA_k_non_expedit_lifted
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ITA_k_vatican_offended }
					has_country_flag = ITA_v_king_consents
					has_country_flag = ITA_v_vienna_mediates
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_idea = ITA_v_the_curia_is_cold }
			modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = ITA_v_curia_steps value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals } }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ITA_k_conciliation_talks

	id = ita_dnc.2080
	title = ita_dnc.2080.t
	desc = ita_dnc.2080.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_parliament
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.2080.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = ITA_k_catholic_group
						has_country_flag = ITA_k_non_expedit_lifted
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 55 compare = less_than } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character = ITA_ottorino_gentiloni } }
			recruit_character = ITA_ottorino_gentiloni
		}
		promote_character = { character = ITA_ottorino_gentiloni ideology = christian_democracy }

				ideology = democratic
				long_name = ITA_v_national_democratic_league_long
				name = ITA_v_national_democratic_league
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = democratic
				long_name = ITA_v_catholic_union_long
				name = ITA_v_catholic_union
			}
		}
		set_cosmetic_tag = ITA_CONC
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = ITA_k_the_conciliation }
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = ITA_k_the_conciliation
				add_idea = ITA_k_the_catholic_kingdom
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_ideas = { ITA_k_the_catholic_kingdom }
		}
		set_country_flag = ITA_dnc_end_catholic_kingdom
		activate_advisor = ITA_filippo_meda
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = ITA_giuseppe_toniolo }
			activate_advisor = ITA_giuseppe_toniolo
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRA }
			FRA = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = small_decrease } }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ITA_vienna_trust value = 5 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = ITA_vienna_trust
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_w_vienna_trust_tt
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.2080.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 3
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 check_variable = { var = ITA_crown value = 60 compare = less_than } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_country_flag = ITA_k_conciliated }
		}
		promote_character = { character = ITA_tommaso_tittoni ideology = conservatism }
